# Skills · M · part 30

838 entries, massive-deposit-data-retrieval to match-rate-delta-tabulation. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **massive-deposit-data-retrieval**: Use when a reproduction task depends on the raw vs preprocessed feature tables or lipid annotation lists referenced in the Bilbao et al. 2021 PreProcessor paper and hosted at ftp://massive.ucsd.edu/MSV000087295.
